Build domain separator\\n        self.domain = build_domain(\\n            chain_id=chain_id,\\n            verifying_contract=self.verifying_contract\\n        )\\n        \\n        # Derive address from private key (in memory only)\\n        from eth_account import Account\\n        self.address = Account.from_key(private_key).address\\n    \\n    def sign_order(\\n        self,\\n        token_id: str,\\n        maker_amount: str,\\n        taker_amount: str,\\n        side: OrderSide,\\n        nonce: int,\\n        fee_rate_bps: str = \\\"100\\\",\\n        expiration: int = 0,\\n        taker: str = ZERO_ADDRESS,\\n        signature_type: SignatureType = SignatureType.EOA\\n    ) -> SignedOrder:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Sign a Polymarket order\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            token_id: ERC1155 token ID of conditional token being traded\\n            maker_amount: Maximum amount maker is willing to spend\\n            taker_amount: Minimum amount taker must pay the maker in return\\n            side: Order side (BUY or SELL)\\n            nonce: Maker's Exchange nonce\\n            fee_rate_bps: Fee rate in basis points (default: \\\"100\\\")\\n            expiration: Unix expiration timestamp (default: 0 = no expiration)\\n            taker: Taker address (default: zero address for open order)\\n            signature_type: Signature type (default: EOA)\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            SignedOrder object with signature\\n            \\n        Security:\\n            - Private key used in memory only\\n            - Never transmitted over network\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Generate random salt for unique order\\n        salt = int(random.random() * time.time() * 1000)\\n        \\n        # Build order message\\n        message = build_order_message(\\n            salt=salt,\\n            maker=self.address,\\n            signer=self.address,\\n            taker=taker,\\n            token_id=token_id,\\n            maker_amount=maker_amount,\\n            taker_amount=taker_amount,\\n            expiration=expiration,\\n            nonce=nonce,\\n            fee_rate_bps=fee_rate_bps,\\n            side=int(side),\\n            signature_type=int(signature_type)\\n        )\\n        \\n        # Sign the order\\n        signature = sign_typed_data(\\n            private_key=self._private_key,\\n            domain=self.domain,\\n            message=message\\n        )\\n        \\n        # Build signed order\\n        return SignedOrder(\\n            salt=salt,\\n            maker=self.address,\\n            signer=self.address,\\n            taker=taker,\\n            tokenId=token_id,\\n            makerAmount=maker_amount,\\n            takerAmount=taker_amount,\\n            expiration=expiration,\\n            nonce=nonce,\\n            feeRateBps=fee_rate_bps,\\n            side=\\\"BUY\\\" if side == OrderSide.BUY else \\\"SELL\\\",\\n            signatureType=int(signature_type),\\n            signature=signature\\n        )\\n    \\n    def verify_order(self, signed_order: SignedOrder) -> bool:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Verify a signed order\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            signed_order: SignedOrder to verify\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            True if signature is valid\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Build message from signed order\\n        message = build_order_message(\\n            salt=signed_order.salt,\\n            maker=signed_order.maker,\\n            signer=signed_order.signer,\\n            taker=signed_order.taker,\\n            token_id=signed_order.tokenId,\\n            maker_amount=signed_order.makerAmount,\\n            taker_amount=signed_order.takerAmount,\\n            expiration=signed_order.expiration,\\n            nonce=signed_order.nonce,\\n            fee_rate_bps=signed_order.feeRateBps,\\n            side=0 if signed_order.side == \\\"BUY\\\" else 1,\\n            signature_type=signed_order.signatureType\\n        )\\n        \\n        # Verify signature\\n        return verify_signature(\\n            signature=signed_order.signature,\\n            domain=self.domain,\\n            message=message,\\n            expected_address=self.address\\n        )\\n    \\n    def to_dict(self, signed_order: SignedOrder) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Convert signed order to dictionary for API submission\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            signed_order: SignedOrder to convert\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            Dictionary representation for Polymarket API\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\\"salt\\\": signed_order.salt,\\n            \\\"maker\\\": signed_order.maker,\\n            \\\"signer\\\": signed_order.signer,\\n            \\\"taker\\\": signed_order.taker,\\n            \\\"tokenId\\\": signed_order.tokenId,\\n            \\\"makerAmount\\\": signed_order.makerAmount,\\n            \\\"takerAmount\\\": signed_order.takerAmount,\\n            \\\"expiration\\\": signed_order.expiration,\\n            \\\"nonce\\\": signed_order.nonce,\\n            \\\"feeRateBps\\\": signed_order.feeRateBps,\\n            \\\"side\\\": signed_order.side,\\n            \\\"signatureType\\\": signed_order.signatureType,\\n            \\\"signature\\\": signed_order.signature\\n        }\\n</content>\\n<metadata>\\nTotal lines: 259, returned complete file\\n</metadata>\"}], \"action_name\": \"ToolReadFile\", \"message\": \"Action 'ToolReadFile' completed successfully\"}", "rejected": false}

WHERE source = 'binance') AS binance\\nON poly.ts >= binance.ts\\nWHERE binance.ts IS NOT NULL\\n    AND delta_ms <= {time_window_ms}\\n```\\n\\n### CLI Usage:\\n```bash\\n# Run alignment on 1 hour of data\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --hours 1\\n\\n# Specify time range\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --start-time \\\"2026-03-08 10:00:00\\\" --end-time \\\"2026-03-08 11:00:00\\\"\\n\\n# Configure time window\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --time-window-ms 500\\n\\n# Store results in ClickHouse\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --store-results\\n```\\n\\n### Files Created:\\n- `backend/sync/__init__.py`\\n- `backend/sync/time_aligner.py`\\n- `tests/test_time_aligner.py`\\n- `scripts/test_time_aligner.py`\\n\\n### Files Modified:\\n- `tasks.json`\\n\\n### Test Results:\\n- \\u2705 14 unit tests pass\\n- \\u2705 ASOF JOIN query building verified\\n- \\u2705 Statistics calculation verified\\n- \\u2705 Query result parsing verified\\n\\n### Note:\\nFull integration testing requires Docker Desktop running with ClickHouse. Created `backend/sync/time_aligner.py` - Main time aligner module with:\\n   - `TimeAligner` async class for ASOF JOIN time alignment\\n   - `TimeDeltaStats` model for statistics (mean, median, std_dev, percentiles)\\n   - `MatchedPair` model for individual matched trade pairs\\n   - `AlignmentResult` model for alignment operation results\\n   - ASOF JOIN query builder for millisecond-precision matching\\n   - Statistics calculation functions\\n   - CLI entry point with argparse\\n\\n3. Created `tests/test_time_aligner.py` - Unit tests with 14 test cases:\\n   - Test models (TimeDeltaStats, MatchedPair, AlignmentResult)\\n   - Test ASOF JOIN query building\\n   - Test query result parsing\\n   - Test statistics calculation\\n   - Test context manager\\n\\n4. Created `scripts/test_time_aligner.py` - Integration test script:\\n   - Inserts sample market data for testing\\n   - Runs time aligner on 1 hour of data\\n   - Outputs average time delta statistics\\n\\n### Key Features:\\n- **ASOF JOIN Query**: Finds closest Binance trade for each Polymarket trade\\n- **Delta Calculation**: `Delta_T = Poly_TS - Binance_TS` in milliseconds\\n- **Statistics**: Mean, median, std_dev, min, max, P25, P75, P95\\n- **Configurable Time Window**: Maximum time difference for matching\\n- **Result Storage**: Option to store alignment results in ClickHouse\\n\\n### ASOF JOIN Query Structure:\\n```sql\\nSELECT\\n    poly.ts AS poly_ts,\\n    binance.ts AS binance_ts,\\n    toFloat64(poly.ts - binance.ts) * 1000 AS delta_ms,\\n    ...\\nFROM (SELECT ... WHERE source = 'polymarket') AS poly\\nASOF LEFT JOIN (SELECT ... WHERE source = 'binance') AS binance\\nON poly.ts >= binance.ts\\nWHERE binance.ts IS NOT NULL\\n    AND delta_ms <= {time_window_ms}\\n```\\n\\n### CLI Usage:\\n```bash\\n# Run alignment on 1 hour of data\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --hours 1\\n\\n# Specify time range\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --start-time \\\"2026-03-08 10:00:00\\\" --end-time \\\"2026-03-08 11:00:00\\\"\\n\\n# Configure time window\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --time-window-ms 500\\n\\n# Store results in ClickHouse\\npython -m backend.sync.time_aligner --store-results\\n```\\n\\n### Files Created:\\n- `backend/sync/__init__.py`\\n- `backend/sync/time_aligner.py`\\n- `tests/test_time_aligner.py`\\n- `scripts/test_time_aligner.py`\\n\\n### Files Modified:\\n- `tasks.json`\\n\\n### Test Results:\\n- \\u2705 14 unit tests pass\\n- \\u2705 ASOF JOIN query building verified\\n- \\u2705 Statistics calculation verified\\n- \\u2705 Query result parsing verified\\n\\n### Note:\\nFull integration testing requires Docker Desktop running with ClickHouse.
